## Requested outcome and correction

The user requires durable idle-lead wakeup and bounded maintenance: ask active
threads to pause, wait a persisted configurable deadline (default90s), interrupt
remaining work, activate normally and send stable resume messages. Preserve
queues/goals/conversations, Stop, recovery and stopped-client restoration. The
responsible thread decides child restart. Strict MCP/history/accounting/mail
prerequisites and automatic child restart remain rejected. Local operators are
trusted; remote clients remain untrusted. The user's duration concern keeps the
next path bounded: source/quicks/consolidation/review, normal publication and one
original packaged CI, without another local preparation cycle.

Design6633, architect fffea413, identifies a source defect: terminal-sync admitted
an owned idle root without establishing its current-generation RecoveryStore
permission, and Ruby checked held status before admission. Exact7540 CI42371509165
failed during terminal restoration with zero participants, ten terminal intents,
different source/destination epochs and settled continuations. Raw log SHA256
7228bb796abf9486b9b7239a9758b479245fed2345bb322d2df6c0b5115f7156 remains retained.
Actual terminal-sync responses and recovery bytes were not exported; neither the
precise historical refusal nor oldd56d causality is uniquely established.

The four-file correction adds a local terminalSync helper to the existing Go
receiver. Under operation and recovery serialization it rechecks active worker,
incomplete restoration, selected package, recorded terminal incarnation and
retained service identity. Cancelled uses original source proof; updated and
source-recovered use recorded destination proof. Missing/mismatched/foreign-
generation recovery records refuse. StopRevision and Automatic=false suppress.
Only the recorded root gains permission; stale-generation members/continuation
are cleared, current-generation permission is preserved, and epoch is checked
at the write boundary. A sentinel aborts the read-only operation update after
the separately serialized recovery effect, preserving the incomplete tail.
Ruby requests owned admission first and then checks fresh held status; suppressed
and admitted-but-held remain effect-free. General work fences, package/token,
readiness, pane and retry behavior remain. Review lock/Stop ordering, idempotence,
crash/error boundaries and identity/epoch proof directly rather than relying on
this summary. No new schema/RPC/native/SDK/input/timing or generic recovery policy.

Owning boundary: Go maintenance receiver/RecoveryStore permission publication and

workspace-maintenance/workspace-host owned tail. Product docs remain unchanged:
docs/workspace-updates.md, docs/dev-sessions.md, docs/portal.md and test/README.md.

User: “we pause active threads, wait for them to settle... if they don't in a given timeout, we interrupt them. we activate the new workspace, we send them command to resume.” The user also explicitly permits child interruption and leaves child restart to the responsible thread. Resume text tells actors to inspect saved state and partial effects before retrying.
